          else g2048TryMove(3);                          // долгое — влево
        }
        g2048OkHolding = false;
      }
      if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
      break;
    }

    case SCR_GAME_REACTION:
      updateReaction();
      if (ev.ok) reactionOnOk();
      if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_MEMORY:
      if (memGameOver) {
        if (!memGameOverShown) { showMemoryGameOver(); memGameOverShown = true; }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) { resetMemory(); memGameOverShown = false; }
      } else {
        if (ev.back) { goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU); break; }
        if (ev.up)   memMoveCursor(-1);
        if (ev.down) memMoveCursor(1);
        if (ev.ok)   memConfirmCursor();
        updateMemory();
      }
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_MINESWEEPER:
      if (mineGameOver) {
        if (!mineGameOverShown) { showMinesweeperGameOver(); mineGameOverShown = true; }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) { resetMinesweeper(); mineGameOverShown = false; }
      } else {
        if (ev.back) { goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU); break; }
        if (ev.up)   mineMoveCursor(-1);
        if (ev.down) mineMoveCursor(1);
        // короткое OK = открыть клетку, долгое OK = поставить/снять флаг —
        // тот же short/long-press приём, что и в 2048/Wi-Fi клавиатуре
        bool okPressedNow = (digitalRead(BTN_OK_PIN) == LOW);
        if (okPressedNow) {
          if (!mineOkHolding) { mineOkHolding = true; mineOkHoldStart = millis(); }
        } else {
          if (mineOkHolding) {
            unsigned long heldFor = millis() - mineOkHoldStart;
            if (heldFor < G2048_HOLD_MS) mineOpenCurrent();
            else mineToggleFlag();
            drawMinesweeperFrame();
          }
          mineOkHolding = false;
        }
        if (mineGameOver) drawMinesweeperFrame();
      }
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_PIPES:
      if (pipeSolved) {
        drawPipesFrame();
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) resetPipes();
      } else {
        if (ev.up)   { pipeMoveCursor(-1); drawPipesFrame(); }
        if (ev.down) { pipeMoveCursor(1);  drawPipesFrame(); }
        if (ev.ok)   { pipeRotateCurrent(); drawPipesFrame(); }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
      }
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_WHACKMOLE:
      if (moleGameOver) {
        if (!moleGameOverShown) { showWhackMoleGameOver(); moleGameOverShown = true; }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) { resetWhackMole(); moleGameOverShown = false; }
      } else {
        if (ev.back) { goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU); break; }
        if (ev.up)   moleMoveCursor(-1);
        if (ev.down) moleMoveCursor(1);
        if (ev.ok)   moleHit();
        updateWhackMole();
      }
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_MAZE:
      if (mazeWon) {
        showMazeWin();
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) { resetMaze(); drawMazeFrame(); }
      } else {
        if (ev.up)   { mazeTryMove(0, -1); drawMazeFrame(); }
        if (ev.down) { mazeTryMove(0, 1);  drawMazeFrame(); }
        // короткое OK = вниз, долгое OK = вверх — тот же приём, что в 2048/Minesweeper
        bool okPressedNow = (digitalRead(BTN_OK_PIN) == LOW);
        if (okPressedNow) {
          if (!mazeOkHolding) { mazeOkHolding = true; mazeOkHoldStart = millis(); }
        } else {
          if (mazeOkHolding) {
            unsigned long heldFor = millis() - mazeOkHoldStart;
            if (heldFor < G2048_HOLD_MS) mazeTryMove(1, 0);
            else mazeTryMove(-1, 0);
            drawMazeFrame();
          }
          mazeOkHolding = false;
        }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
      }
      break;

    case SCR_GAME_DODGER:
      if (dodgeGameOver) {
        if (!dodgeGameOverShown) { showDodgerGameOver(); dodgeGameOverShown = true; }
        if (ev.back) goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU);
        else if (ev.ok) { resetDodger(); dodgeGameOverShown = false; }
      } else {
        if (ev.back) { goToScreen(SCR_GAMES_MENU); break; }
        if (ev.up)   dodgeMovePlayer(-6);
        if (ev.down) dodgeMovePlayer(6);
        updateDodger();
      }
      break;

    default: break;
  }
}
